evidence-modeObserved, sensed, measured, reported, inferred, remembered, simulated, generated, or unknown source mode.
mirativityExpected, unexpected, counterexpected, surprising, or model-revising information status.
identity-modeStrict, material, structural, processual, causal, genealogical, informational, functional, role, or legal continuity.
agency-modeIndividual, delegated, distributed, emergent, centralised, coordinated, or fictionalised agency.
decision-modeVote, command, consensus, market outcome, delegated decision, algorithmic selection, or other decision architecture.
causal-layerImmediate, contributory, enabling, structural, mediated, preventive, triggering, or feedback causal level.
reversibilityReversible, costly-to-reverse, practically irreversible, or physically irreversible status.
effect-orderDirect, second-order, third-order, cascade, or recursively propagated consequence.
feedbackPositive, negative, delayed, stabilising, destabilising, or mixed feedback regime.
boundarySharp, fuzzy, operational, disputed, dynamic, permeable, or scale-dependent boundary status.
mandateRule, vote, delegation, charter, consent, or authority that licences representation or action.
coordinationNone, local, stigmergic, negotiated, centralised, distributed, synchronised, or emergent coordination.
controlCapacity to select, prevent, redirect, repair, or authorise an outcome.
responsibilityTyped allocation of causal, role, control, knowledge, benefit, or remedial responsibility.
ownerEntity governing a module, artefact, resource, definition, or right.
authorityInstitution, person, standard, contract, or capability that validates or permits an action.
methodMeasurement, analysis, inference, retrieval, experimental, or compilation method.
datasetVersioned dataset, table, stream, corpus, or data slice used or produced.
modelScientific, statistical, causal, simulation, machine-learning, or semantic model.
codeVersioned executable artefact or source module referenced by a scientific or operational node.
environmentContainer, hardware, library lock, solver configuration, or execution environment.
queryReference to the query for which a result, proof, diagnostic, or explanation is produced.
returnVariable, field, proposition, proof component, or aggregate requested by a query.
orderDeterministic ordering criterion for query or execution output.
limitResult, time, memory, search, token, or iteration limit.
witnessReplayable example, model, path, trace, or counterexample supporting an execution result.
proofProof object, certificate, derivation, or checkable receipt.
statusAccepted, proposed, derived, rejected, unknown, conflicted, superseded, or other governed state.
coverageScope, completeness policy, revision, exclusions, and method supporting positive or negative conclusions.
missing-reasonUnknown, not measured, withheld, not applicable, unsupported, unavailable, or conflicted reason.
capabilityExplicit permission required for an operational interpreter or external effect.
budgetTime, memory, cost, search, token, energy, or external-service allowance.
determinismDeterministic, seeded, stochastic, nondeterministic, or externally contingent execution contract.
monotonicityMonotonic, antimonotonic, non-monotonic, unknown, or context-dependent semantic behaviour.
effectPure, state-read, state-write, file, network, tool, notification, or other operational effect class.
cacheCacheability and invalidation contract for a node or operator.
severityInformational, warning, error, blocking, or domain-specific priority of a diagnostic or constraint.
priorityRule, default, scheduling, review, or conflict-resolution priority.
whenPremise or condition that activates a rule, constraint, decision, or query.
unlessException or defeater that blocks a default, rule, constraint, or action.
thenConclusion, effect, or output activated by a rule.
alternativeMaterially distinct semantic candidate retained without premature selection.
assumptionExplicit premise introduced by a model, human, method, or profile rather than directly supported by the source.
aliasLocal import or symbol alias resolved to a qualified canonical identity before hashing.
guaranteeDeclared guarantee envelope such as syntactic, source-grounded, model-relative, bounded, proved, or replay-verified.
output-typeDeclared type of the value or semantic node produced by a registered verb.
requiredList or schema object describing parameters that every use of a verb must provide.
optionalList or schema object describing parameters that a verb may accept without changing its identity.
replacementNew node, value, rule, or commitment that supersedes an earlier target.
variableTyped logical or query variable introduced for binding in patterns and rules.
causeEvent, agent, condition, or proposition presented as the cause of a change under a declared causal profile.
purposeGoal or intended function attributed to an action without conflating intention with achieved effect.
relationDeclared relation symbol or relation node used as the content of a higher-order construction.
roleNamed semantic, social, institutional, discourse, or provenance role.
ruleRegistered rule or derivation object used to license an inference.
alternativesCollection of materially distinct semantic candidates retained for comparison or suspended judgement.
politenessRegister, deference, familiarity, face-management, or conventional politeness value.
distanceSpatial, temporal, social, causal, epistemic, or threshold-relative distance under a named scale.
rankSocial, institutional, taxonomic, preference, or result rank under an explicit ordering.
relationshipTyped interpersonal, kin, institutional, contractual, or discourse relationship.
communityCommunity, speech group, institution, or governance body relative to which a convention applies.
directionAbsolute, intrinsic, relative, temporal, causal, or scalar direction.
processProcess node or process description relevant to continuation, unboundedness, or transformation.
intentionDeclared intention, plan, or goal state of an agent, kept distinct from effect and responsibility.
mechanismCausal, operational, biological, social, or computational mechanism linking source and target.
delayTemporal lag between cause and effect, signal and response, or action and consequence.
incentiveReward, penalty, target, metric, or institutional pressure capable of changing behaviour.
resultResult artefact or outcome produced, compared, replicated, or consumed by another node.

APPENDIX C

Candidate Operator Library

The table is the main implementation output of the analysis in the book. It combines ordinary semantic operations with operators motivated by English grammar, cross-linguistic constructions, epistemology, scientific practice, social organisation, and systems reasoning. Each retained path corresponds to a `.sle` contract defining parameters, types, semantic conditions, examples, tests, and compatible interpreters.

TermExplanation
slenglish/core/import.sleLoads a versioned module into the current package and binds an optional local alias. Required: module version. Optional: namespace alias content-digest profile. Output: module handle.
slenglish/core/define-verb.sleDeclares a typed semantic operator and its executable contract. Required: name output-type. Optional: module version required optional cardinality determinism monotonicity effect profile description. Output: verb schema.
slenglish/core/define-type.sleDeclares a semantic or data type. Required: name. Optional: parent module version description. Output: type handle.
slenglish/core/define-role.sleDeclares a reusable parameter or semantic role. Required: name. Optional: type cardinality description module version. Output: role handle.
slenglish/core/define-enum.sleDeclares a closed or versioned set of symbolic values. Required: name value. Optional: description module version. Output: enum handle.
slenglish/core/define-unit.sleDeclares a unit, dimension, conversion relation, and authority. Required: name. Optional: type basis authority version description. Output: unit handle.
slenglish/core/alias.sleBinds a local readable name to a stable qualified symbol. Required: name target. Optional: context version. Output: alias handle.
slenglish/core/entity.sleCreates or registers a typed entity without asserting further contingent facts. Required: type. Optional: id name label description source content-digest context. Output: entity value.
slenglish/core/variable.sleCreates a typed variable for patterns, rules, and queries. Required: type. Optional: name description context. Output: variable value.
slenglish/core/context.sleCreates a world, discourse, legal, quoted, hypothetical, or execution context. Required: name. Optional: parent world jurisdiction profile valid-time source coverage description. Output: context handle.
slenglish/core/assert.sleCommits a proposition as accepted in a named context. Required: proposition context. Optional: source source-span valid-time authority status. Output: commitment.
slenglish/core/deny.sleCommits explicit negative polarity for a proposition in a named context. Required: proposition context. Optional: source source-span valid-time authority status. Output: negative commitment.
slenglish/core/claim.sleRecords that a source or agent advances a proposition without automatically accepting it. Required: proposition source. Optional: source-span source-depth evidence-mode confidence valid-time context status. Output: claim.
slenglish/core/hypothesize.slePlaces a proposition in a hypothesis context for testing rather than acceptance. Required: proposition context. Optional: source source-span confidence assumption profile status. Output: hypothesis commitment.
slenglish/core/assume.sleIntroduces an explicit premise for a bounded analysis. Required: proposition context. Optional: source basis valid-time status. Output: assumption commitment.
slenglish/core/quote.sleRecords the exact or controlled content attributed to a source or speaker. Required: proposition source. Optional: source-span speaker addressee time context language. Output: quotation.
slenglish/core/retract.sleWithdraws a prior commitment while preserving historical provenance. Required: target. Optional: source basis time authority context. Output: retraction.
slenglish/core/supersede.sleDeclares that a new node replaces an earlier node for specified uses. Required: target replacement. Optional: basis time context version. Output: supersession relation.
slenglish/core/rule.sleDeclares a strict, default, causal, temporal, probabilistic, or profile-specific derivation. Required: when then profile. Optional: unless priority context source description. Output: rule handle.
slenglish/core/constraint.sleDeclares a validation, consistency, cardinality, numeric, temporal, or policy obligation. Required: when profile. Optional: unless severity context source description. Output: constraint handle.
slenglish/core/query.sleCreates a first-class query with explicit context, guarantee, coverage, and return contract. Required: pattern return. Optional: context profile coverage guarantee order limit budget description. Output: query handle.
slenglish/core/invoke.sleRequests execution of a registered operational circuit under explicit capability. Required: target capability. Optional: value context budget environment effect source. Output: invocation request.
slenglish/core/explain.sleRequests a source-linked explanation of a result, proof, conflict, or dependency. Required: target. Optional: query profile limit language perspective. Output: explanation request.
slenglish/core/diagnose.sleCreates a typed diagnostic for ambiguity, loss, invalidity, conflict, or unsupported semantics. Required: target status. Optional: source-span severity basis alternative description. Output: diagnostic.
knowledge/logic/and.sleConstructs a conjunction whose operands must be interpreted under the same named profile. Required: proposition proposition. Optional: context order. Output: conjunctive proposition.
knowledge/logic/or.sleConstructs an inclusive disjunction unless a profile declares another mode. Required: proposition proposition. Optional: context mode order. Output: disjunctive proposition.
knowledge/logic/not.sleConstructs explicit logical negation rather than absence or failure to derive. Required: proposition. Optional: context profile. Output: negative proposition.
knowledge/logic/imply.sleConstructs material, strict, or profile-specific implication without itself asserting either side. Required: premise conclusion. Optional: profile context. Output: implication proposition.
knowledge/logic/equivalent.sleDeclares equivalence under a named semantics and scope. Required: source target. Optional: profile context basis strength. Output: equivalence proposition.
knowledge/logic/each.sleBinds universal quantification over a typed domain. Required: variable type. Optional: when context profile. Output: quantified proposition.
knowledge/logic/some.sleBinds existential quantification over a typed domain. Required: variable type. Optional: when context profile. Output: quantified proposition.
knowledge/logic/none.sleStates that no member of a typed, covered domain satisfies a pattern. Required: variable type pattern. Optional: coverage context profile. Output: negative quantified proposition.
knowledge/logic/exactly.sleConstrains the number of matches to an exact cardinality. Required: pattern cardinality. Optional: coverage context profile. Output: cardinality constraint.
knowledge/logic/at-least.sleConstrains the number or degree of matches to a lower bound. Required: pattern min. Optional: coverage context profile unit. Output: lower-bound constraint.
knowledge/logic/at-most.sleConstrains the number or degree of matches to an upper bound. Required: pattern max. Optional: coverage context profile unit. Output: upper-bound constraint.
knowledge/entity/classify.slePlaces an entity in a type without claiming strict identity with the type. Required: source type. Optional: context valid-time basis source-span. Output: classification proposition.
knowledge/entity/exist.sleStates existence in an explicit world, interval, or model. Required: source context. Optional: world valid-time evidence-mode source-span. Output: existence proposition.
knowledge/entity/equal.sleStates strict substitutive identity under an explicitly named profile. Required: source target. Optional: context basis valid-time. Output: identity proposition.
knowledge/entity/different.sleStates non-identity under an explicitly named profile. Required: source target. Optional: context basis valid-time. Output: difference proposition.
knowledge/entity/name.sleAssociates an entity with a label, identifier, or name in a language and scope. Required: source label. Optional: language context valid-time authority. Output: naming proposition.
knowledge/property/state.sleAttributes a typed state or property value to an entity. Required: source value. Optional: type unit valid-time context basis. Output: state proposition.
knowledge/property/change.sleRepresents change of a property between explicit states or values. Required: source from to. Optional: time duration cause context. Output: change proposition.
knowledge/relation/relate.sleConstructs a declared relation when no more specialised verb is available. Required: source target type. Optional: context valid-time basis degree. Output: relation proposition.
knowledge/relation/compare.sleCompares two values or entities along an explicit dimension and comparator. Required: source target basis. Optional: mode value unit context. Output: comparison proposition.
knowledge/relation/depend-on.sleRepresents semantic, causal, build, resource, or logical dependency. Required: source target. Optional: mode strength context valid-time. Output: dependency proposition.
knowledge/relation/associate.sleRepresents an explicitly non-causal association with method and strength. Required: source target. Optional: method basis strength confidence context dataset. Output: association proposition.
language/event/occur.sleCreates an event with explicit participants, time, and context. Required: type. Optional: agent actor patient theme location time duration context source-span. Output: event proposition.
language/event/act.sleRepresents an action by an agent or actor toward an optional target. Required: agent. Optional: target patient theme instrument purpose time location context. Output: action proposition.
language/event/transfer.sleRepresents transfer of a theme between origin and destination or recipient. Required: theme. Optional: agent origin destination recipient instrument time purpose context. Output: transfer event.
language/event/move.sleRepresents spatial, virtual, organisational, or state-space movement. Required: theme origin destination. Optional: agent path instrument time duration context. Output: motion event.
language/event/create.sleRepresents bringing an entity, record, artefact, or state into existence. Required: agent target. Optional: instrument method time context source. Output: creation event.
language/event/destroy.sleRepresents termination or destruction with explicit patient and causal context. Required: agent patient. Optional: instrument method time context reversibility. Output: destruction event.

language/event/perceive.sleRepresents perception by an experiencer through an optional sense or instrument. Required: experiencer stimulus. Optional: instrument mode time location context. Output: perception event.
language/event/experience.sleRepresents an internal state or experience without imposing external observability. Required: experiencer value. Optional: stimulus degree time context. Output: experience event.
language/event/change.sleRepresents a transition of an entity, relation, or system between states. Required: target from to. Optional: causer time duration reversibility context. Output: change event.

language/event/distribute.sleDistributes an action or relation across members, times, or objects. Required: proposition distribution. Optional: collective member interval context. Output: distributed event.
language/event/reciprocate.sleRepresents mutually directed instances of a relation among participants. Required: relation collective. Optional: member time context. Output: reciprocal proposition.
language/event/reflexive.sleRepresents a participant filling two co-referential semantic roles. Required: agent. Optional: patient theme role context. Output: reflexive event.
language/event/causative.sleAdds an explicit causer and causee to an event structure. Required: causer proposition. Optional: causee instrument causal-layer time context. Output: causative event.
language/event/applicative.slePromotes a beneficiary, instrument, location, or recipient into a core event role. Required: proposition target mode. Optional: context. Output: applicative event.
language/event/antipassive.sleSuppresses or backgrounds a patient while foregrounding an agent and activity. Required: proposition agent. Optional: patient aspect context. Output: antipassive event.
language/event/middle.sleRepresents an event whose affected participant is central without a fully external agent. Required: patient proposition. Optional: agent aspect context. Output: middle event.

language/time/locate.sleLocates an event, state, claim, or rule at an instant or interval. Required: target time. Optional: context valid-time transaction-time remoteness. Output: temporal proposition.

knowledge/evidence/quote.sleLinks a proposition to exact or controlled quoted language. Required: proposition source source-span. Optional: speaker addressee language time context. Output: quotation evidence.

science/research/simulation.sleDeclares a simulation run under a model, parameters, and environment. Required: model environment. Optional: dataset code value uncertainty time context. Output: simulation artefact.
science/research/result.sleRecords an output with method, uncertainty, and source artefacts. Required: value. Optional: unit uncertainty method dataset model code context. Output: result artefact.
science/research/claim.sleDeclares a scientific claim with scope, modality, and support relation. Required: proposition. Optional: source source-span model dataset confidence valid-time context. Output: scientific claim.
science/research/support.sleLinks evidence or results to a claim under a named method. Required: source target. Optional: basis strength confidence context. Output: support relation.
science/research/refute.sleLinks evidence or a counterexample to a challenged claim. Required: source target. Optional: basis witness strength context. Output: refutation relation.
science/research/replicate.sleRecords a replication attempt and its relation to an earlier experiment or result. Required: source target. Optional: method dataset environment result time context. Output: replication relation.
science/research/review.sleCreates a review artefact targeting a claim, method, dataset, or package. Required: source-agent target. Optional: status severity proposition source-span time context. Output: review artefact.
science/research/challenge.sleRecords a specific objection to a premise, inference, identity, scope, or evidence relation. Required: source-agent target proposition. Optional: basis source-span severity context. Output: challenge artefact.
science/research/retract.sleRecords scientific withdrawal of a claim or artefact with reasons and authority. Required: target authority. Optional: basis time source context. Output: scientific retraction.
knowledge/provenance/derive.sleStates that an artefact or proposition was derived from one or more inputs. Required: source target. Optional: method code model time context. Output: derivation relation.
knowledge/provenance/attribute.sleAttributes an artefact, action, or claim to an agent. Required: source target. Optional: role time authority context. Output: attribution relation.
knowledge/provenance/generate.sleStates that an activity generated an entity or artefact. Required: source target. Optional: time environment context. Output: generation relation.
knowledge/provenance/use.sleStates that an activity or analysis used an entity or artefact. Required: source target. Optional: time version context. Output: usage relation.
knowledge/provenance/quote.sleLinks a quotation or claim to exact source material. Required: source target source-span. Optional: language context. Output: quotation provenance.
knowledge/provenance/cite.sleLinks a claim or document to a bibliographic or data source. Required: source target. Optional: source-span basis context. Output: citation relation.
knowledge/provenance/version.sleLinks artefact versions and their content digests. Required: source target version. Optional: content-digest time context. Output: version-provenance relation.
knowledge/provenance/span.sleLinks a semantic node to an exact lexical, table, figure, code, or data span. Required: source source-span. Optional: content-digest context. Output: span relation.
execution/workflow/depend.sleDeclares that a build target depends on another node or artefact. Required: source target. Optional: mode version context. Output: build dependency.
execution/workflow/build.sleRequests or records construction of a target from its dependencies. Required: target. Optional: profile environment budget time context. Output: build event.
execution/workflow/invalidate.sleMarks cached or derived nodes invalid after a dependency change. Required: target source. Optional: basis time context. Output: invalidation event.
execution/workflow/recompute.sleRequests or records recomputation under a new epoch. Required: target. Optional: source environment budget time context. Output: recomputation event.
execution/reasoning/derive.sleRecords a derived proposition with premises, rule, profile, and proof. Required: conclusion premise. Optional: rule profile proof context. Output: derivation result.
execution/reasoning/entail.sleStates that a target follows under a named formal semantics and coverage. Required: premise conclusion profile. Optional: proof coverage context. Output: entailment result.
execution/reasoning/contradict.sleStates incompatibility between propositions under a named profile. Required: proposition proposition. Optional: proof context severity. Output: contradiction result.
execution/reasoning/may.sleStates that a proposition holds in at least one admissible interpretation. Required: proposition context. Optional: coverage witness profile. Output: may result.
execution/reasoning/must.sleStates that a proposition holds in all admissible interpretations represented by the analysis. Required: proposition context. Optional: coverage proof profile. Output: must result.
execution/reasoning/cannot.sleStates that a proposition holds in no admissible interpretation represented by the analysis. Required: proposition context. Optional: coverage proof profile. Output: cannot result.
execution/reasoning/unknown.sleRecords inability to decide because of unsupported semantics, coverage, budget, or evidence. Required: target. Optional: missing-reason coverage budget context. Output: unknown result.
execution/reasoning/conflicted.sleRecords simultaneous support for a proposition and its explicit negation. Required: proposition. Optional: source alternative context. Output: conflicted result.
execution/reasoning/prove.sleRequests or records proof under a named calculus or checker. Required: target profile. Optional: premise proof budget context. Output: proof result.
execution/reasoning/explain.sleProduces a source- and proof-linked explanation of a result. Required: target. Optional: query return proof source-span language context. Output: explanation result.
execution/reasoning/witness.sleRecords a replayable model, trace, input, or path supporting possibility or violation. Required: target witness. Optional: environment source-span context. Output: witness result.
execution/reasoning/counterexample.sleRecords a replayable case refuting a universal claim or constraint. Required: target witness. Optional: environment source-span context. Output: counterexample result.
execution/query/whether.sleAsks whether a proposition is true, false, unknown, or conflicted in a context. Required: pattern context. Optional: profile coverage guarantee return budget. Output: truth-status query.
execution/query/select.sleReturns bindings satisfying a pattern. Required: pattern return. Optional: context profile coverage guarantee order limit budget. Output: selection query.
execution/query/count.sleCounts matches under explicit classifier and coverage. Required: pattern. Optional: return context profile coverage guarantee limit. Output: count query.
execution/query/compare.sleCompares results, models, versions, or scenarios along named dimensions. Required: source target basis. Optional: return context profile guarantee. Output: comparison query.
execution/query/why.sleRequests derivation, provenance, causal path, or assumption explanation. Required: target. Optional: return context profile guarantee limit. Output: explanation query.
execution/query/what-if.sleEvaluates a proposition under explicit counterfactual changes. Required: target assumption. Optional: alternative context profile guarantee budget. Output: counterfactual query.
execution/runtime/validate.sleRuns syntax, schema, type, profile, provenance, and policy validation. Required: target. Optional: profile severity context. Output: validation result.
execution/runtime/reject.sleRecords deterministic rejection and its exact cause. Required: target basis. Optional: severity source-span context. Output: rejection result.
execution/runtime/diagnose.sleProduces a machine-readable diagnostic for a stopped or degraded execution. Required: target status. Optional: basis severity source-span alternative context. Output: runtime diagnostic.
language/english/clause/declarative.sleConstructs a proposition presented as a declarative clause without itself asserting that proposition. Required: proposition. Optional: source-span context information-structure. Output: clause object.
language/english/clause/interrogative.sleRepresents an interrogative form and its requested variable, polarity, or alternative set. Required: question. Optional: addressee context source-span. Output: question object.
language/english/clause/imperative.sleRepresents an imperative form while keeping command force distinct from operational authority. Required: action addressee. Optional: speaker strength context source-span. Output: directive object.
language/english/voice/active.sleRecords an active-voice surface alignment where relevant to source analysis. Required: proposition. Optional: subject agent source-span. Output: voice annotation.
language/english/voice/passive.sleNormalises passive alignment while recording whether the agent is expressed, unknown, backgrounded, or strategically omitted. Required: proposition patient. Optional: agent omission-reason source-span. Output: voice-qualified proposition.
language/english/aspect/progressive.slePresents an event from within its unfolding interval. Required: event reference-time. Optional: boundedness interruption context. Output: aspect-qualified proposition.
language/english/aspect/perfect.sleRelates a prior situation to a later reference point without assuming completion or current relevance beyond the selected profile. Required: event reference-time. Optional: result-state context. Output: aspect-qualified proposition.
language/english/aspect/habitual.sleRepresents a recurrent disposition or pattern rather than one actual occurrence. Required: event-pattern holder. Optional: frequency interval exceptions context. Output: habitual proposition.
language/english/tense/past.slePlaces reference time before the evaluation time under an explicit temporal profile. Required: proposition evaluation-time. Optional: remoteness interval context. Output: tense-qualified proposition.
language/english/tense/non-past.sleRecords English non-past morphology while requiring another operator or context to distinguish present, generic, scheduled, or future interpretation. Required: proposition. Optional: evaluation-time interpretation context. Output: tense annotation.
language/english/modality/modal.sleDisambiguates an English modal auxiliary into epistemic, deontic, dynamic, predictive, or logical mode. Required: proposition mode. Optional: holder authority degree source-span context. Output: modal proposition.
language/english/negation/sentential.sleRepresents sentence negation with explicit structural scope. Required: proposition. Optional: scope focus context source-span. Output: negative proposition.
language/english/negation/not-all.sleRepresents narrow negation of a universal claim rather than universal negation. Required: quantified-proposition. Optional: context source-span. Output: scope-qualified negative proposition.
language/english/reference/article-indefinite.sleRecords an indefinite noun phrase and its introduction of a discourse referent or generic reading. Required: type. Optional: variable quantity genericity source-span context. Output: reference object.
language/english/reference/article-definite.sleRecords a definite description with its identification basis rather than assuming uniqueness globally. Required: description. Optional: referent familiarity uniqueness-scope source-span context. Output: reference object.
language/english/reference/pronoun.sleRepresents a pronoun and candidate antecedents, agreement features, discourse role, and confidence. Required: source-span. Optional: antecedent alternative person number gender perspective context. Output: reference candidate.
language/english/reference/expletive.sleMarks syntactic subjects such as weather it or existential there that do not denote ordinary semantic agents. Required: form clause. Optional: source-span context. Output: expletive annotation.
language/english/discourse/ellipsis.sleRecords omitted recoverable material and candidate reconstructions. Required: source-span alternative. Optional: antecedent context confidence. Output: ellipsis interpretation.
language/english/discourse/presuppose.sleRecords content conventionally treated as backgrounded or already accommodated without automatically asserting it in every context. Required: proposition trigger. Optional: holder context source-span. Output: presupposition object.
language/english/discourse/implicate.sleRecords a defeasible pragmatic enrichment distinct from literal content. Required: proposition basis. Optional: speaker addressee context confidence cancellable source-span. Output: implicature candidate.
language/english/information/topic.sleMarks the discourse topic associated with a proposition. Required: target proposition. Optional: contrast context source-span. Output: information-structure annotation.
language/english/information/focus.sleMarks focused material and the relevant alternative set. Required: target proposition. Optional: alternatives contrast context source-span. Output: focus annotation.
language/english/information/cleft.sleRepresents cleft constructions with explicit focus and presupposed background. Required: focus background. Optional: source-span context. Output: information-structure object.
language/english/coordination/and.sleRepresents coordination while allowing the relation to be specified as addition, sequence, result, contrast, or joint participation. Required: proposition proposition. Optional: relation order context source-span. Output: coordinated proposition.
language/english/coordination/or.sleRepresents inclusive or exclusive alternatives with explicit scope and exhaustivity. Required: proposition proposition. Optional: mode exhaustive context source-span. Output: disjunctive proposition.
language/english/subordination/conditional.sleRepresents condition and consequent with explicit modality, counterfactual status, and temporal relation. Required: condition consequence. Optional: mode world context source-span. Output: conditional proposition.
language/english/subordination/causal.sleRecords that an English because or related clause presents an explanatory or causal relation, without deciding the causal profile automatically. Required: source target. Optional: causal-layer evidence context source-span. Output: causal candidate.
language/english/subordination/concessive.sleRepresents a concession relation between an expectation and a proposition. Required: expectation proposition. Optional: holder degree context source-span. Output: concessive proposition.
language/english/report/direct-speech.slePreserves quoted wording, speaker, addressee, time, and source span without asserting quoted content. Required: content speaker. Optional: addressee time source context. Output: quotation object.
language/english/report/indirect-speech.sleRepresents reported content with tense, person, and perspective adjustments kept distinct from acceptance. Required: proposition source-agent. Optional: perspective time source-depth context source-span. Output: report object.
language/english/nominalisation/event.sleExpands an event nominalisation into a candidate event frame and records omitted roles. Required: nominal source-span. Optional: agent patient theme time cause alternatives context. Output: event candidate.
language/english/compound/noun-noun.sleRepresents the underspecified semantic relation inside an English noun compound and preserves alternatives when consequence changes. Required: modifier head. Optional: relation alternative context source-span. Output: compound interpretation.
language/english/comparison/comparative.sleRepresents comparison along an explicit dimension, standard, and degree. Required: source target basis. Optional: degree unit context source-span. Output: comparison proposition.
language/english/comparison/superlative.sleRepresents a maximum or minimum relative to an explicit comparison set and coverage. Required: source basis comparison-set. Optional: order coverage context source-span. Output: superlative proposition.
language/english/possession/genitive.sleTreats the English genitive as an underspecified relation requiring ownership, kinship, authorship, part, control, association, or another declared interpretation. Required: possessor possessed. Optional: relation alternatives context source-span. Output: possession candidate.
language/english/phrasal-verb.sleResolves a verb-particle construction to a declared semantic operator while preserving literal alternatives where relevant. Required: verb particle source-span. Optional: operator alternative context. Output: lexical-semantic candidate.
language/english/light-verb.sleRepresents constructions such as take a walk or make a decision through an event operator rather than treating the light verb as the principal action. Required: verb nominal. Optional: agent roles context source-span. Output: event candidate.
language/english/control/subject.sleRecords the understood subject of a non-finite complement when controlled by a matrix argument. Required: controller embedded-proposition. Optional: relation context source-span. Output: control relation.
language/english/raising.sleRecords a raising construction in which a surface subject is not assigned its semantic role by the matrix predicate. Required: source embedded-proposition. Optional: evidential-mode context source-span. Output: raising analysis.
language/english/attachment/alternative.slePreserves materially distinct prepositional, relative-clause, or modifier attachments as explicit alternatives. Required: source-span alternative. Optional: ranking confidence context. Output: interpretation set.

APPENDIX D

English and Cross-Linguistic Constructions as Executable Operators

This table records how the English baseline and the cross-linguistic survey are translated into executable distinctions. Each row identifies a grammatical or conceptual phenomenon and the SLEnglish operator pattern that can represent it. The mapping is refined through grammar consultation, corpus analysis, speaker or specialist expertise where relevant, translation tests, and reasoning tasks.

TermExplanation

language/english/clause/imperativeEnglish baseline mapping. Represents an imperative form while keeping command force distinct from operational authority. Required: action addressee. Optional: speaker strength context source-span. Output: directive object.
language/english/voice/activeEnglish baseline mapping. Records an active-voice surface alignment where relevant to source analysis. Required: proposition. Optional: subject agent source-span. Output: voice annotation.
language/english/voice/passiveEnglish baseline mapping. Normalises passive alignment while recording whether the agent is expressed, unknown, backgrounded, or strategically omitted. Required: proposition patient. Optional: agent omission-reason source-span. Output: voice-qualified proposition.
language/english/aspect/progressiveEnglish baseline mapping. Presents an event from within its unfolding interval. Required: event reference-time. Optional: boundedness interruption context. Output: aspect-qualified proposition.
language/english/aspect/perfectEnglish baseline mapping. Relates a prior situation to a later reference point without assuming completion or current relevance beyond the selected profile. Required: event reference-time. Optional: result-state context. Output: aspect-qualified proposition.

Grammatical evidentialityLanguages with grammatical evidentials require or strongly conventionalise distinctions such as direct observation, report, and inference. Representative scholarship includes Aikhenvald and the WALS evidentiality maps. SLEnglish mapping: knowledge/evidence/observe.sle, knowledge/evidence/report.sle, knowledge/evidence/infer.sle, knowledge/evidence/remember.sle, knowledge/evidence/measure.sle; profiles may require evidence-mode and source-depth.
MirativityMirative systems mark information as unexpected, newly integrated, or contrary to a model. The category is debated and often overlaps evidentiality. SLEnglish mapping: knowledge/epistemic/expect.sle, knowledge/epistemic/counterexpect.sle, knowledge/epistemic/surprise.sle with mirativity, holder, prior model, and degree.
Inclusive versus exclusive first-person pluralMany languages distinguish a “we” that includes the addressee from one that excludes them. English normally leaves this optional. SLEnglish mapping: language/reference/inclusive-we.sle and language/reference/exclusive-we.sle, with explicit speaker, addressee, members, and collective scope.
Dual, trial, paucal, and associative numberLanguages may grammatically distinguish exactly two, exactly three, a small plurality, or an individual plus associates. SLEnglish mapping:
language/number/dual.sle, language/number/paucal.sle, and language/number/associative.sle; classifiers and coverage specify what is counted.language/number/trial.sle,
Numeral classifiersClassifier languages require counting through a classifying unit that can encode shape, animacy, arrangement, or conventional individuation. SLEnglish mapping: science/quantity/classifier.sle and science/quantity/count.sle with classifier, identity criterion, and coverage.
Alienable and inalienable possessionPossessive systems often distinguish transferable ownership from kin, body-part, constitutive, or otherwise inalienable relations. SLEnglish
mapping: language/possession/inalienable.sle, language/possession/kin.sle,language/possession/alienable.sle,
language/possession/body-part.sle, language/possession/stewardship.sle.and
Absolute spatial framesLanguages documented by Levinson and others may habitually use cardinal, uphill/downhill, riverine, or seaward/landward frames rather than body-relative left/right. SLEnglish mapping: language/space/north-of.sle, uphill, upriver, seaward, and related operators with explicit anchor and scale.
Remoteness tenseSome tense systems distinguish recent and remote pasts or near and remote futures. SLEnglish mapping: language/time/recent-past.sle,
language/time/remote-past.sle, language/time/remote-future.sle, with interval definitions supplied by a context profile.language/time/near-future.sle,
Rich aspect systemsPerfective, imperfective, progressive, habitual, perfect, prospective, iterative, durative, punctual, telic, and atelic distinctions expose event contour rather than only chronological location. SLEnglish mapping: the corresponding language/time/* operators.
Switch-referenceClause-chaining systems can mark whether adjacent clauses share a
centralparticipant.SLEnglishmapping:
language/discourse/same-reference.sle language/discourse/different-reference.sle with clause nodes, participant anchors, and source spans.and
LogophoricityLogophoric systems identify forms interpreted relative to the consciousness or speech perspective of a reported individual. SLEnglish mapping: language/reference/logophor.sle and language/perspective/logophoric.sle with explicit holder and context.
EgophoricitySome systems mark privileged personal authority or involvement, especially for one’s own states and intentional actions. SLEnglish mapping: language/perspective/egophoric.sle with holder, proposition, evidence mode, and context; it does not automatically imply truth.
Obviation and proximate statusAlgonquian and other discourse systems can distinguish a more central third person from less central third persons. SLEnglish
mapping: language/reference/obviative.sle, used to stabilise reference and perspective.language/reference/proximate.sleand
Direct–inverse alignmentDirect–inverse systems mark whether an event follows or reverses a
person/animacy language/perspective/pivot.sle plus explicit event roles; experimental operator libraries may add alignment/direct.sle and alignment/inverse.sle if corpus evidence justifies them.hierarchy.SLEnglishmapping:
Applicative constructionsApplicatives promote beneficiaries, instruments, locations, or recipients into core event structure. SLEnglish mapping: language/event/applicative.sle or specialised event verbs with explicit promoted role.
Antipassive constructionsAntipassives foreground agent and activity while reducing patient prominence or definiteness. SLEnglish mapping: language/event/antipassive.sle with explicit underlying proposition, agent, optional patient, and aspect.
Causative constructionsLanguages grammaticalise adding a causer and causee in varied ways. SLEnglish mapping: language/event/causative.sle and the knowledge/causation/* library; the representation must distinguish causing an event from merely asserting causal relation.
Middle and reflexive voiceMiddle and reflexive systems alter participant structure and affectedness. SLEnglish mapping: language/event/middle.sle and language/event/reflexive.sle with explicit co-reference or agent suppression.
PluractionalitySome languages mark event plurality, distribution, repetition, or participant plurality morphologically. SLEnglish mapping:
language/event/repeat.sle, language/time/iterative.sle, and language/number/distributive.sle.language/event/distribute.sle,
Serial verb constructionsSerial verbs encode tightly linked actions, paths, aspects, or participant sharing without ordinary coordination. SLEnglish mapping: language/event/serialise.sle with ordered proposition nodes and declared shared roles.
Medial verbs and clause chainingClause-chaining languages use medial forms to mark non-final clauses, participant continuity, temporal relations, and discourse dependency. SLEnglish mapping: discourse switch-reference operators plus time and workflow dependencies.
Realis and irrealisLanguages may mark whether an event is presented as actual, intended, hypothetical, feared, possible, or counterfactual. SLEnglish
mapping: knowledge/modality/irrealis.sle, knowledge/modality/counterfactual.sle, and explicit contexts.knowledge/modality/realis.sle,
Quotative and reportative constructionsMany languages distinguish exact quotation, report, hearsay, and
speaker language/discourse/quote-direct.sle, language/discourse/report-indirect.sle, knowledge/evidence/quote.sle, and knowledge/evidence/report.sle.perspective.SLEnglishmapping:
Honorific and humilific systemsSpeech may grammatically encode relative rank, respect, familiarity, or self-lowering. SLEnglish mapping: social/relation/honor.sle, social/relation/humble-self.sle, social/relation/address-deferential.sle, and social/relation/address-familiar.sle.
Taboo and avoidance registersSome communities maintain special vocabularies or registers for particular kin relations, rituals, or contexts. SLEnglish mapping: social/relation/taboo.sle and social/relation/register.sle with community authority and validity scope.
Noun classes and animacy hierarchiesLanguages classify nouns by gender, animacy, shape, social status, or other semantic and grammatical criteria. SLEnglish mapping: typed entity classifications and explicit classifier profiles; no one hierarchy is assumed universal.
IdeophonesIdeophones can depict sensory scenes, movement, texture, intensity, or manner with high expressive precision. SLEnglish should retain the original form as data and may map interpretable dimensions through perception or event operators without claiming exhaustive translation.
Olfactory lexiconsResearch on languages such as Jahai and Maniq shows that odours can be named with greater abstraction than English stereotypes suggest. SLEnglish mapping: knowledge/evidence/sense.sle with olfactory mode plus domain smell operators, while culturally specific lexemes remain versioned concepts.
Relational nounsSome languages lexicalise relations that English expresses as possessed nouns or loose prepositions. SLEnglish mapping: dedicated relation or possession verbs rather than generic ownership.
Body-part incorporationIncorporation can encode recurrent participant structure inside the verb. SLEnglish mapping: language/event/incorporate.sle plus language/possession/body-part.sle and explicit affected participant.
Distributive versus collective predicationA plural proposition may apply to each member, to the group jointly, or to a subgroup. SLEnglish mapping: language/number/distributive.sle, language/number/collective.sle, social/collective/* constructors, and explicit quantifier scope.
Inclusive imperative and hortativeLanguages can distinguish commands including speaker, addressee, or third parties. SLEnglish mapping: participant-set constructors combined with language/speech-act/command.sle or a hortative profile.
Evidential source depthNatural languages rarely grammaticalise an arbitrary number of testimony hops, but the distinction is crucial for science and online information. SLEnglish invention: source-depth and knowledge/provenance/derive.sle chains attached to evidence verbs.
Process identityNo single grammatical category universally solves the identity of clouds, rivers, flames, organisms, or institutions. SLEnglish conceptual invention: knowledge/identity/process.sle with causal path, boundary grade, scale, interval, and degree.
Collective agency architectureNatural languages readily personify states, firms, markets, and societies. SLEnglish conceptual invention: collective constructors and social/agency/delegated.sle, distributed, emergent, or centralized to distinguish decision from outcome.
Responsibility vectorsOrdinary grammar compresses causation, knowledge, control, benefit, mandate, and remediation into “responsible.” SLEnglish conceptual invention: social/responsibility/* operators that allocate these dimensions separately.
Reversibility gradeOrdinary verbs rarely mark whether an action can be reversed cheaply, only at high cost, or not at all. SLEnglish conceptual
invention: knowledge/risk/irreversible.sle, and reversibility parameters.knowledge/risk/reversible.sle,
Causal depthEveryday causal language mixes trigger, direct mechanism, enabling condition, structural cause, and feedback. SLEnglish mapping: distinct knowledge/causation/* verbs and causal-layer.
Scale addressA proposition may be true at individual, organisational, population, or ecosystem scale and misleading elsewhere. SLEnglish conceptual invention: knowledge/system/scale.sle, micro, meso, and macro.
Boundary gradeClouds, communities, species, and institutions may have fuzzy, operational, dynamic, or contested boundaries. SLEnglish conceptual invention: knowledge/identity/boundary.sle and the boundary parameter.
Unknown versus unknowableEnglish can express the distinction lexically but rarely requires it.
SLEnglish knowledge/epistemic/unknowable.sle, coverage, and missing-reason.knowledge/epistemic/unknown.sle,
Absence versus non-existenceMissing records, zero measured quantity, unavailable evidence, and non-existence are different. SLEnglish mapping: explicit missing-value atoms, science/quantity/zero.sle, knowledge/entity/exist.sle, and coverage-sensitive knowledge/logic/none.sle.
Goodhart pressureMetrics used as targets can change the system they describe.
SLEnglish knowledge/system/goodhart-pressure.sle linked to incentive, target, and observed distortion.conceptualinvention:
Posterity referenceFuture people cannot participate in current pronoun systems or contracts but may bear effects. SLEnglish conceptual invention: knowledge/system/future-generation.sle with time, uncertainty, benefit, and burden.
Option value and opportunity costOrdinary language often hides the value of retaining alternatives and the best forgone path. SLEnglish mapping:
social/decision/option-value.sle social/decision/opportunity-cost.sle.and
Unknown unknownsA system may know that its current question set is incomplete without enumerating what is missing. SLEnglish conceptual invention: knowledge/risk/unknown-unknown.sle with an explicit frontier and coverage statement.
